```

**Output:**
```
def build_design_context(config, pdk=None, name=None):
    """Turn a parsed netlist ``config`` into a DesignContext (§11).

    Extracts, in addition to the plain device/net lists:
      * power / ground nets
      * differential pairs   (same type, shared source, matched geometry)
      * current mirrors      (shared gate, one device diode-connected)
      * generic matched sets (identical model + geometry on a shared gate)
      * critical / sensitive nets
      * placement clusters

    Nothing here invents geometry; it only reads what the netlist states.
    """
    from mbg.design_context import (
        DesignContext, Device, Net, MatchingGroup, SymmetryConstraint,
    )

    subckt = next((c for c in config.get("components", [])
                   if c.get("type") == "subcircuit"), None)
    ctx = DesignContext(name=name or (subckt or {}).get("subcir", "top"))
    ctx.pdk = pdk
    if pdk is not None:
        from mbg.pdk_rules import get_rules
        ctx.rules = get_rules(pdk)
    if subckt:
        ctx.top_ports = list(subckt.get("port", []))

    # ── devices ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    for comp in config.get("components", []):
        if comp.get("type") != "device":
            continue
        p = comp.get("parameters", {}) or {}
        kind = _device_kind(comp.get("model"), comp.get("name"))
        nf = int(_num(p.get("nf"), 1) or 1)
        m = int(_num(p.get("m"), 1) or 1)
        w_total = _num(p.get("w") or p.get("r_width") or p.get("c_width"))
        l_val = _num(p.get("l") or p.get("r_length") or p.get("c_length"))
        dev = Device(
            name=comp["name"],
            model=comp.get("model", ""),
            kind=kind,
            terminals={t: n for t, n in (comp.get("nodes") or {}).items() if n != "-"},
            width=w_total,
            length=l_val,
            fingers=max(1, nf),
            multipliers=max(1, m),
            params=dict(p),
        )
        # SPICE W is the TOTAL device width; glayout wants per-finger width.
        if dev.is_mos and w_total:
            dev.finger_width = w_total / float(max(1, nf) * max(1, m))
        else:
            dev.finger_width = w_total
        _validate_device(dev, comp)
        ctx.add_device(dev)

    # ── nets ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    for dev in ctx.devices.values():
        for term, net_name in dev.terminals.items():
            net = ctx.nets.get(net_name)
            if net is None:
                low = net_name.lower()
                net = Net(name=net_name,
                          is_power=low in _POWER_NAMES,
                          is_ground=low in _GROUND_NAMES)
                ctx.add_net(net)
            net.terminals.append((dev.name, term))

    for net in ctx.nets.values():
        if net.is_power:
            ctx.power_nets.add(net.name)
            net.weight = 1.5
        elif net.is_ground:
            ctx.ground_nets.add(net.name)
            net.weight = 1.5

    # ── deep n-well isolation ───────────────────────────────────────
    # GF180MCU has no separate DNW transistor model, so isolation cannot be
    # requested by model name. It is visible in the connectivity instead: an
    # NMOS whose bulk sits on something other than the global ground can only
    # be built with a deep n-well, and that is exactly the case where someone
    # is using the body effect on purpose.

```

def spice_to_gds_with_checks(netlist_input, gds_path=None, mode="analog",
                             add_labels=True, legacy=False, verbosity=0,
                             placement_config=None, router_config=None,
                             power_rails=True, rail_layer="met5",
                             write_views=True):
    """SPICE -> GDS with DRC, LVS and PEX. The primary entry point.

    Since v0.2 this drives the DesignContext flow (analog-aware placement plus
    the DRC-aware grid router with internal connectivity verification), because
    the original shape-router path could not label top-level pins correctly and
    failed LVS pin matching on every reference design.

    Measured on the four reference blocks (inverter, ring oscillator, 5T OTA,
    StrongArm comparator):

        legacy path   0/4 pass   (LVS: top-level cell failed pin matching)
        this path     4/4 pass   (DRC clean, LVS match, 0 opens, 0 shorts)

    Pass ``legacy=True`` to run the original implementation unchanged.

    Returns the same keys as before — ``outdir``, ``gds_path``, ``svg_path``,
    ``cell_name``, ``drc``, ``lvs``, ``pex``, ``all_pass`` — plus ``context``,
    ``verification`` and ``metrics`` from the new flow.
    """
    if legacy:
        return spice_to_gds_with_checks_legacy(
            netlist_input, gds_path=gds_path, mode=mode, add_labels=add_labels)
    return spice_to_gds_with_checks_ctx(
        netlist_input, gds_path=gds_path, mode=mode, add_labels=add_labels,
        verbosity=verbosity, placement_config=placement_config,
        router_config=router_config, power_rails=power_rails,
        rail_layer=rail_layer, write_views=write_views)


_PASSIVE_PARAM_NAMES = {
    "XR": {"w": "r_width", "l": "r_length"},
    "XC": {"w": "c_width", "l": "c_length"},
}


def _canonical_passive_params(netlist: str) -> str:
    """Rewrite ``W=``/``L=`` on passive instances to the PDK's own names.

    Magic extracts a poly resistor as ``r_width``/``r_length`` and a MIM as
    ``c_width``/``c_length``. Netgen compares properties *by name*, so a
    schematic written the natural way (``W=1u L=4u``) reports
    "Property r_width in circuit1 has no matching property in circuit2" even
    though the device and its dimensions are identical.

    This renames a parameter to its canonical spelling and nothing else. It
    does not touch connectivity, values, or device types — unlike net
    merging (see ``checks._merge_schematic_nets``), it cannot make a broken
    layout look correct, because a wrong dimension still mismatches.
    """

```

{
  "content": "\"\"\"Build a comparator variant layout and measure its systematic post-layout\ninput-referred offset via a differential-input ramp on the extracted netlist.\n\"\"\"\nimport sys\nimport os\nimport time\n\nsys.path.insert(0, \"/home/huda/opensource-project/Microelectronic-Block-Generator/src\")\nsys.path.insert(0, \"/home/huda/opensource-project/Microelectronic-Block-Generator/results/deepseek_strongarm_comparator\")\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport sa_measure as M\n\nOUT = \"/home/huda/opensource-project/Microelectronic-Block-Generator/results/deepseek_strongarm_comparator/probe\"\n\n\ndef netlist(inp_w=\"10u\", inp_nf=5, inp_l=\"0.6u\", tail_w=\"8u\", tail_nf=4):\n    return f\"\"\"\n.subckt strongarm_comparator VDD VSS INP INN CLK OUTP OUTN\nXM1  TAIL CLK VSS VSS nfet_03v3 L=0.5u W={tail_w} nf={tail_nf}\nXM2  D1 INP TAIL VSS nfet_03v3 L={inp_l} W={inp_w} nf={inp_nf}\nXM3  D2 INN TAIL VSS nfet_03v3 L={inp_l} W={inp_w} nf={inp_nf}\nXM4  OUTN OUTP D1 VSS nfet_03v3 L=0.5u W=6u nf=3\nXM5  OUTP OUTN D2 VSS nfet_03v3 L=0.5u W=6u nf=3\nXM6  OUTN OUTP VDD VDD pfet_03v3 L=0.5u W=6u nf=3\nXM7  OUTP OUTN VDD VDD pfet_03v3 L=0.5u W=6u nf=3\nXM8  OUTN CLK VDD VDD pfet_03v3 L=0.5u W=2u nf=1\nXM9  OUTP CLK VDD VDD pfet_03v3 L=0.5u W=2u nf=1\nXM10 D1 CLK VDD VDD pfet_03v3 L=0.5u W=2u nf=1\nXM11 D2 CLK VDD VDD pfet_03v3 L=0.5u W=2u nf=1\n.ends\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef systematic_offset(pex_netlist, workdir):\n    \"\"\"Flip point of the extracted comparator on a +-30 mV ramp.\"\"\"\n    ramp = list(np.arange(-0.030, 0.030 + 1e-12, 0.005))\n    res, cycles, _ = M.run_combined(pex_netlist, vd_list=ramp,\n                                    workdir=workdir, tstep=\"0.5n\")\n    signs = [+1 if c[\"diff_settled\"] > 0 else -1 for c in cycles]\n    for k in range(1, len(signs)):\n        if signs[k - 1] != signs[k]:\n            return 0.5 * (ramp[k - 1] + ramp[k])\n    return (ramp[-1] if signs[-1] == 1 else ramp[0])\n\n\ndef run_variant(name, nl, verbosity=0):\n    wd = os.path.join(OUT, name)\n    os.makedirs(wd, exist_ok=True)\n    t0 = time.time()\n    from mbg.pipeline import spice_to_gds_with_checks\n    r = spice_to_gds_with_checks(nl, verbosity=verbosity)\n    print(f\"[{name}] build elapsed {round(time.time()-t0,1)}s \"\n          f\"all_pass={r['all_pass']} gds={os.path.basename(r['gds_path'])}\")\n    if not r[\"all_pass\"]:\n        print(f\"[{name}] verification failed: {r.get('verification')}\")\n        return None\n    pex_path = (r.get(\"pex\") or {}).get(\"pex_path\")\n    if not pex_path or not os.path.isfile(pex_path):\n        print(f\"[{name}] no pex netlist\")\n        return None\n    off = systematic_offset(open(pex_path).read(), os.path.join(wd, \"ramp\"))\n    print(f\"[{name}] systematic post-layout offset = {off*1e3:+.2f} mV\")\n    return off\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    variants = {}\n    for arg in sys.argv[1:]:\n        name, _, _ = arg.partition(\"=\")\n        variants[name] = arg.split(\"=\", 1)[1]\n    if not variants:\n        print(\"usage: offset_probe.py name='W=20u nf=10 L=0.6u' [name2=...]\")\n        sys.exit(2)\n    for name, spec in variants.items():\n        parts = {}\n        for tok in spec.split():\n            k, _, v = tok.partition(\"=\")\n            parts[k] = v\n        nl = netlist(inp_w=parts.get(\"W\", \"10u\"), inp_nf=int(parts.get(\"nf\", 5)),\n                     inp_l=parts.get(\"L\", \"0.6u\"),\n                     tail_w=parts.get(\"TW\", \"8u\"), tail_nf=int(parts.get(\"TNF\", 4)))\n        run_variant(name, nl)\n",

    # is using the body effect on purpose.

    for dev in ctx.devices.values():

            ctx.deep_nwell_flags[dev.name] = True

    # ── analog structure recognition ────────────────────────────────
    def _geom_key(d):
        return (d.kind, d.model.lower(), d.width, d.length, d.fingers, d.multipliers)

    mos = [d for d in ctx.devices.values() if d.is_mos]

    # differential pairs: same type + geometry, shared SOURCE, distinct gates
    by_source = {}
    for d in mos:
        by_source.setdefault(d.terminals.get("source"), []).append(d)
    pair_id = 0
    paired = set()
    for src, devs in by_source.items():
        if not src or src.lower() in _POWER_NAMES | _GROUND_NAMES:
            continue                     # a shared rail is not a tail node
        for i in range(len(devs)):
            for j in range(i + 1, len(devs)):
                a, b = devs[i], devs[j]
                if a.name in paired or b.name in paired:
                    continue
                if _geom_key(a) != _geom_key(b):
                    continue
                if a.terminals.get("gate") == b.terminals.get("gate"):
                    continue
                if a.terminals.get("drain") == b.terminals.get("drain"):
                    continue
                pair_id += 1
                gname = f"diffpair_{pair_id}"
                ctx.matching_groups[gname] = MatchingGroup(
                    gname, "diff_pair", [a.name, b.name], symmetric=True)
                ctx.symmetry_constraints.append(
                    SymmetryConstraint(gname, a.name, b.name, "vertical"))
                ctx.clusters[gname] = [a.name, b.name]
                ctx.differential_pairs.append(
                    (a.terminals.get("gate"), b.terminals.get("gate")))
                paired.update({a.name, b.name})
                # gate nets of a diff pair are the sensitive inputs
                for g in (a.terminals.get("gate"), b.terminals.get("gate")):
                    if g and not ctx.is_power_or_ground(g):
                        ctx.sensitive_nets.add(g)
                        ctx.nets[g].is_sensitive = True
                        ctx.nets[g].weight = max(ctx.nets[g].weight, 2.0)

    # current mirrors: shared gate net where one device is diode-connected
    by_gate = {}
    for d in mos:
        by_gate.setdefault(d.terminals.get("gate"), []).append(d)
    mir_id = 0
    for gate, devs in by_gate.items():
        if not gate or len(devs) < 2:
            continue
        diode = [d for d in devs if d.terminals.get("drain") == gate]
        if not diode:
            continue
        same_kind = [d for d in devs if d.kind == diode[0].kind]
        if len(same_kind) < 2:
            continue
        mir_id += 1
        gname = f"mirror_{mir_id}"
        names = [d.name for d in same_kind]
        ctx.matching_groups[gname] = MatchingGroup(
            gname, "current_mirror", names,
            symmetric=len({_geom_key(d) for d in same_kind}) == 1)
        ctx.clusters[gname] = names
        ctx.critical_nets.add(gate)
        ctx.nets[gate].is_critical = True
        ctx.nets[gate].weight = max(ctx.nets[gate].weight, 2.0)

    # generic matched sets: identical devices sharing a gate, not yet grouped
    grouped = {n for g in ctx.matching_groups.values() for n in g.devices}
    gen_id = 0
    for gate, devs in by_gate.items():
        cand = [d for d in devs if d.name not in grouped]
        if len(cand) < 2:
            continue
        buckets = {}
        for d in cand:
            buckets.setdefault(_geom_key(d), []).append(d)
        for key, group in buckets.items():
            if len(group) < 2:
                continue
            gen_id += 1
            gname = f"matched_{gen_id}"
            names = [d.name for d in group]
            ctx.matching_groups[gname] = MatchingGroup(gname, "generic", names, True)
            ctx.clusters[gname] = names
            grouped.update(names)

}

  "newString": "    # cross-coupled latch pairs: same geometry, gates swapped with drains.\n    # A regenerative latch (e.g. the cross-coupled pair of a comparator) has\n    # two devices whose gate net is the other device's drain net:\n    #     XA dA gA sA b,  XB dB gB sB b   with  gA == dB and gB == dA.\n    # They share no source and no gate, so neither the diff-pair nor the\n    # current-mirror detector sees them — but they are the most matching-\n    # critical devices in a latch, so leaving them ungrouped lets the placer\n    # break the very symmetry the layout needs.\n    grouped = {n for g in ctx.matching_groups.values() for n in g.devices}\n    cc_id = 0\n    for i in range(len(mos)):\n        for j in range(i + 1, len(mos)):\n            a, b = mos[i], mos[j]\n            if a.name in grouped or b.name in grouped:\n                continue\n            if a.kind != b.kind or _geom_key(a) != _geom_key(b):\n                continue\n            ga = a.terminals.get(\"gate\"); da = a.terminals.get(\"drain\")\n            gb = b.terminals.get(\"gate\"); db = b.terminals.get(\"drain\")\n            if not (ga and gb and da and db):\n                continue\n            if ga == db and gb == da:\n                cc_id += 1\n                gname = f\"latch_{cc_id}\"\n                ctx.matching_groups[gname] = MatchingGroup(\n                    gname, \"generic\", [a.name, b.name], True)\n                ctx.symmetry_constraints.append(\n                    SymmetryConstraint(gname, a.name, b.name, \"vertical\"))\n                ctx.clusters[gname] = [a.name, b.name]\n                grouped.update({a.name, b.name})\n                # the output nets of a latch are sensitive\n                for n in (da, db):\n                    if n and not ctx.is_power_or_ground(n):\n                        ctx.sensitive_nets.add(n)\n                        ctx.nets[n].is_sensitive = True\n                        ctx.nets[n].weight = max(ctx.nets[n].weight, 2.0)\n\n    # generic matched sets: identical devices sharing a gate, not yet grouped\n    gen_id = 0\n    for gate, devs in by_gate.items():\n        cand = [d for d in devs if d.name not in grouped]",

{
  "command": "cd /home/huda/opensource-project/Microelectronic-Block-Generator/results/deepseek_strongarm_comparator; source /home/huda/.mbg/activate.sh; $MBG_VENV/bin/python - > /tmp/opencode/sa_probe/diag.log 2>&1 <<'EOF'\nimport sys, re\nsys.path.insert(0, \"/home/huda/opensource-project/Microelectronic-Block-Generator/src\")\nsys.path.insert(0, \".\")\nimport numpy as np\nimport sa_measure as M\npex = open(\"strongarm_comparator/strongarm_comparator.pex.spice\").read()\n# identify D1/D2 via latch sources\nD1=D2=None\nfor l in pex.splitlines():\n    m=re.match(r\"\\s*X\\d+\\s+(\\S+)\\s+(OUTP|OUTN)\\s+(\\S+)\\s+VSS\\s+nfet_03v3\", l)\n    if m and m.group(3) not in (\"OUTP\",\"OUTN\",\"VSS\",\"VDD\"):\n        if m.group(2)==\"OUTN\": D1=m.group(3)\n        else: D2=m.group(3)\nprint(\"D1=\",D1,\"D2=\",D2)\ndef capv(v):\n    v=v.strip(); mult=1.0\n    if v.endswith('f'): mult=1e-15; v=v[:-1]\n    elif v.endswith('p'): mult=1e-12; v=v[:-1]\n    return float(v)*mult\n# total cap per node (coupling caps)\nfrom collections import defaultdict\ntot=defaultdict(float)\nfor l in pex.splitlines():\n    if re.match(r\"^C\\d+\\s\",l):\n        p=l.split(); v=capv(p[3]); tot[p[1]]+=v; tot[p[2]]+=v\ncD1,cD2=tot[D1],tot[D2]\nprint(f\"coupling caps: D1={cD1*1e15:.3f} D2={cD2*1e15:.3f} fF  diff={ (cD2-cD1)*1e15:.3f} fF\")\n# which node has excess? add compensating cap to the lighter node to equalize\nexcess_node = D2 if cD2>cD1 else D1\nlight_node = D1 if excess_node==D2 else D2\nextra = abs(cD2-cD1)\n# insert a compensating cap light_node<->gnd of magnitude extra\nlines = pex.splitlines()\n# find insertion point before .ends\nout=[]\nadded=False\nfor l in lines:\n    out.append(l)\n    if not added and l.strip().lower().startswith(\".ends\"):\n        out.append(f\"Cbal {light_node} 0 {extra:.6g}\")\n        added=True\npex_bal = \"\\n\".join(out)\n# measure offset on balanced\nramp=list(np.arange(-0.030,0.030+1e-12,0.005))\ndef offset_of(pex_text, wd):\n    res,cycles,_=M.run_combined(pex_text, vd_list=ramp, workdir=wd, tstep=\"0.5n\")\n    signs=[+1 if c[\"diff_settled\"]>0 else -1 for c in cycles]\n    for k in range(1,len(signs)):\n        if signs[k-1]!=signs[k]:\n            return 0.5*(ramp[k-1]+ramp[k])\n    return ramp[-1] if signs[-1]==1 else ramp[0]\noff0=offset_of(pex,\"sim/diag0\")\noff1=offset_of(pex_bal,\"sim/diag1\")\nprint(f\"original offset = {off0*1e3:+.2f} mV\")\nprint(f\"balanced offset = {off1*1e3:+.2f} mV  (added {extra*1e15:.3f} fF to {light_node})\")\nEOF\ncat /tmp/opencode/sa_probe/diag.log"
}

{

}

Actually — let me reconsider the mechanism once more. The offset is reduced by weak tail (lower Vov → higher gain → smaller input-referred latch offset). This confirms the input-referred offset is dominated by the LATCH internal asymmetry divided by the integration gain.

The integration gain ≈ ΔV_trigger/(Vov/2). To maximize it:
- Minimize Vov (weak tail, big input pair).
- Maximize ΔV_trigger: the D-node voltage swing before the latch triggers. The latch triggers when the D nodes drop to ~VDD - |Vtp| (PMOS turn-on) or when the cross-coupled NMOS conduct. If the D nodes discharge MORE before triggering, ΔV_trigger is larger → more gain. The trigger level is set by the latch PMOS/NMOS thresholds.

Hmm, can I make the latch trigger LATER (larger ΔV_trigger)? The D-node discharge stops when the latch regenerates. If the latch is WEAKER (smaller latch devices), it triggers later → more integration → more gain → smaller offset. But weaker latch → slower regeneration (but the delay budget is 5ns).

Wait — but the "fast_latch" variant (latch... actually fast_latch was the default). Let me test a WEAKER latch (smaller W) to increase ΔV_trigger.

  "filePath": "/home/huda/opensource-project/Microelectronic-Block-Generator/results/deepseek_strongarm_comparator/run_strongarm.py",
  "newString": "# ── netlist ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n# Strong-Arm latch comparator, 11 MOS, flat, single subckt. Ports exactly\n# VDD VSS INP INN CLK OUTP OUTN. Convention: INP > INN -> OUTP high.\n# Sizing notes (chosen for this PDK, not copied — validated by post-layout\n# offset probing, see the design report):\n#   - tail XM1 W=2u L=0.5u: a deliberately *weak* clock switch. The tail\n#     current sets the input-pair overdrive, and the input-referred offset\n#     of a Strong-Arm latch is (Vov/2)*(dC/C) at the regenerative nodes. A\n#     weak tail keeps Vov small, which is what keeps the systematic\n#     post-layout offset below the 5 mV decision requirement despite the\n#     unavoidable routing imbalance of the automatic grid router.\n#   - input pair XM2/XM3 W=16u L=0.6u nf=8: large area restores the\n#     transconductance the weak tail would otherwise take away, so the\n#     comparator still clears the 5 ns decision budget and the 1.0 V low\n#     common-mode edge. Long-enough L keeps Vth mismatch within the\n#     10 mV 1-sigma offset target.\n#   - latch NMOS XM4/XM5 W=6u L=0.5u, latch PMOS XM6/XM7 W=6u L=0.5u:\n#     cross-coupled regenerative pair, short L for fast regeneration.\n#   - precharge XM8-XM11 W=2u L=0.5u: outputs + internal nodes to VDD.\nNETLIST = f'''\n.lib \"{lib_path}\" typical\n.subckt {CELL} VDD VSS INP INN CLK OUTP OUTN\nXM1  TAIL CLK VSS VSS nfet_03v3 L=0.5u W=2u nf=1\nXM2  D1 INP TAIL VSS nfet_03v3 L=0.6u W=16u nf=8\nXM3  D2 INN TAIL VSS nfet_03v3 L=0.6u W=16u nf=8\nXM4  OUTN OUTP D1 VSS nfet_03v3 L=0.5u W=6u nf=3\nXM5  OUTP OUTN D2 VSS nfet_03v3 L=0.5u W=6u nf=3\nXM6  OUTN OUTP VDD VDD pfet_03v3 L=0.5u W=6u nf=3\nXM7  OUTP OUTN VDD VDD pfet_03v3 L=0.5u W=6u nf=3\nXM8  OUTN CLK VDD VDD pfet_03v3 L=0.5u W=2u nf=1\nXM9  OUTP CLK VDD VDD pfet_03v3 L=0.5u W=2u nf=1\nXM10 D1 CLK VDD VDD pfet_03v3 L=0.5u W=2u nf=1\nXM11 D2 CLK VDD VDD pfet_03v3 L=0.5u W=2u nf=1\n.ends\n'''.strip()",
  "oldString": "# ── netlist ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n# Strong-Arm latch comparator, 11 MOS, flat, single subckt. Ports exactly\n# VDD VSS INP INN CLK OUTP OUTN. Convention: INP > INN -> OUTP high.\n# Sizing notes (chosen for this PDK, not copied):\n#   - input pair XM2/XM3  W=10u L=0.6u (L short enough that the comparator\n#     clears the 5 ns decision budget even at the low end of the common-mode\n#     range and after extraction; area still large enough that Vth mismatch\n#     keeps the 1-sigma offset target within reach)\n#   - tail XM1 W=8u L=0.5u (low-impedance clock switch: pulls the shared\n#     source node down fast, which is what lets the input pair integrate at\n#     low common-mode input)\n#   - latch NMOS XM4/XM5 W=6u L=0.5u, latch PMOS XM6/XM7 W=6u L=0.5u\n#     (fast regenerative pull-down/pull-up; short L so the latch clears the\n#     delay budget comfortably even at the slow ss/-40C/3.0V corner)\n#   - precharge XM8-XM11 W=2u L=0.5u (outputs + internal nodes to VDD;\n#     50 ns reset half-period is ~50x the needed time)\nNETLIST = f'''\n.lib \"{lib_path}\" typical\n.subckt {CELL} VDD VSS INP INN CLK OUTP OUTN\nXM1  TAIL CLK VSS VSS nfet_03v3 L=0.5u W=8u nf=4\nXM2  D1 INP TAIL VSS nfet_03v3 L=0.6u W=10u nf=5\nXM3  D2 INN TAIL VSS nfet_03v3 L=0.6u W=10u nf=5\nXM4  OUTN OUTP D1 VSS nfet_03v3 L=0.5u W=6u nf=3\nXM5  OUTP OUTN D2 VSS nfet_03v3 L=0.5u W=6u nf=3\nXM6  OUTN OUTP VDD VDD pfet_03v3 L=0.5u W=6u nf=3\nXM7  OUTP OUTN VDD VDD pfet_03v3 L=0.5u W=6u nf=3\nXM8  OUTN CLK VDD VDD pfet_03v3 L=0.5u W=2u nf=1\nXM9  OUTP CLK VDD VDD pfet_03v3 L=0.5u W=2u nf=1\nXM10 D1 CLK VDD VDD pfet_03v3 L=0.5u W=2u nf=1\nXM11 D2 CLK VDD VDD pfet_03v3 L=0.5u W=2u nf=1\n.ends\n'''.strip()"

_Thinking:_

The flow is complete. Let me write the final summary to the user. I'll be concise but complete, covering the outcome, key numbers, what was changed, and the deliverables.

## Result: `SUCCESS` — TAPEOUT_READY

The MBG full-auto flow completed for the Strong-Arm comparator in `results/deepseek_strongarm_comparator/`.

### Outcome
Pre-layout and PEX (post-layout) **all 9 specs PASS**; the sign-off gate is fully green (pre-layout specs, PEX specs, dual-engine DRC clean, LVS match, PEX extraction, final GDS/PEX netlist, no CRITICAL findings, reviews complete, report).

| Metric (post-layout) | Result | Target |
|---|---|---|
| Decision time (5 mV, worst polarity) | **1.32 ns** | ≤ 5 ns |
| Differential swing | **3.30 V** | ≥ 2.97 V |
| Avg current @ 10 MHz | **6.7 µA** | ≤ 500 µA |
| Static current between comparisons | **0.13 nA** | ≈ 0 |
| Correct decisions at ±5…±100 mV | **10/10** | required |
| Reset/precharge + regeneration | **10/10** | required |
| ICMR | **0.9 – 2.7 V** | ⊇ 1.0–2.3 V |

### Characterization (in report + `characterization.json`)
- **Decision time vs |VIN_DIFF|** (both polarities): 5 mV → 0.93 ns, 10 mV → 0.85 ns, 25 mV → 0.77 ns, 50 mV → 0.70 ns, 100 mV → 0.66 ns (schematic).
- **PVT** — all 45 cells (5 corners × 3 temps × 3 VDD) pass: t_dec 0.61–1.45 ns, 10/10 correct, full swing.
- **Mismatch Monte Carlo (60 runs): mean −0.42 mV, 1σ 3.33 mV, 3σ 9.98 mV** — within the 10 mV target.

### Key engineering points
- **Independent sizing**, not copied: deliberately **weak tail (2 µm)** + **large input pair (16 µm/8f, L=0.6 µm)**. Post-layout probing showed the auto-router's routing imbalance creates a ~12–17 mV systematic input offset (fails ±5 mV); a weak tail keeps input-pair overdrive low, cutting that offset to ≤2.5 mV while the large pair restores gm for the 1.0 V low-VCM edge and the 5 ns budget.
- **Framework fix**: added cross-coupled-latch detection to `src/mbg/spice_parser.py` so XM4/XM5 and XM6/XM7 are recognized as matched groups (they share no source/gate, so they previously weren't). This improved placement symmetry; all parser tests still pass.
- Toolchain: Magic + KLayout DRC sign-off PASS, netgen LVS match, PEX extraction clean, layout 50×41 µm.

### Deliverables
`final/` (GDS, PEX netlist, DRC/LVS reports, iteration history), `full_auto_result.json`, `history.json`, `review_history.json`, `final_design_report.md`, `characterization.json`, plus `run_strongarm.py`/`sa_measure.py` for reproduction.

Git status: `src/mbg/spice_parser.py` modified (parser enhancement); the results directory is untracked. Note: `prompt-test/CMP.md` is another agent's file, not mine. I did not commit anything.
